Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The physical variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 902 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 911 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_902(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 902. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^911(S, F) is trivial. QED.
